2c:T317a0,<h2 id="tanstack-start-10-정식-출시-개요--사용법--마이그레이션-가이드">TanStack Start 1.0 정식 출시: 개요 · 사용법 · 마이그레이션 가이드<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#tanstack-start-10-정식-출시-개요--사용법--마이그레이션-가이드"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h3 id="️-한-줄-요약">✍️ 한 줄 요약<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#️-한-줄-요약"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>TanStack Start는 TanStack Router 위에 SSR · 스트리밍 · 서버 함수 · 미들웨어를 얹은 풀스택 React 프레임워크이고, 2026년 10월 13일 1.0(npm <code>1.169</code>)으로 정식 안정화됐습니다.</strong> 기본값은 "서버에서 그리고 브라우저에서 하이드레이션하는 익숙한 앱"이고 서버가 필요한 일만 <code>createServerFn</code> 같은 명시적인 API로 넘긴다는 점이, 서버 컴포넌트가 기본인 Next.js App Router와 가장 크게 갈리는 지점입니다.</p>
</blockquote>
<h4 id="요약">요약<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#요약"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong>출시</strong>: 2026년 10월 13일 TanStack 블로그 공지("TanStack Start 1.0 is Stable"). 첫 안정 패키지는 <code>@tanstack/react-start@1.169.0</code>이고, 글을 쓰는 시점(2026-10-22)의 최신은 <code>1.169.3</code>입니다. '1.0'은 제품 버전 이름이라 <code>@tanstack/react-start@1.0</code>이 아니라 <code>1.169</code> 이상을 설치합니다(RC 기간에도 이미 1.1xx였기 때문에 숫자만으로는 RC와 정식을 구분할 수 없습니다).</li>
<li><strong>정체성</strong>: 라우팅은 <strong>전부 TanStack Router</strong>가 맡고, Start는 그 위에 풀 도큐먼트 SSR · 스트리밍 · 서버 함수(타입 안전 RPC) · 서버 라우트(API) · 미들웨어/컨텍스트 · 클라이언트/서버 빌드 · 유니버설 배포를 더합니다. 공식 홈페이지 기준으로 React와 Solid를 지원합니다.</li>
<li><strong>빌드 도구</strong>: <strong>Vite 8 이상</strong> 또는 <strong>Rsbuild 2</strong>. Node는 22.12 이상입니다.</li>
<li><strong>실행 모델</strong>: 모든 코드는 기본이 <strong>isomorphic</strong>(서버·클라이언트 양쪽에서 실행)입니다. 라우트 <code>loader</code>도 첫 요청에선 서버에서, 이후 내비게이션에선 브라우저에서 돕니다. 비밀값과 DB 접근은 서버 함수나 <code>.server.ts</code> 파일로 경계를 그어야 합니다.</li>
<li><strong>렌더링 선택지</strong>: 라우트별 <code>ssr: true | 'data-only' | false</code>(Selective SSR), 앱 전체 SPA 모드, 정적 프리렌더링, HTTP 캐시 헤더 기반 ISR을 한 프레임워크 안에서 고릅니다.</li>
<li><strong>RSC</strong>: <strong>실험적 · opt-in</strong> 입니다. Next.js처럼 기본 컴포넌트 모델이 아니라, 서버 함수가 서버에서 렌더링한 UI를 돌려주고 클라이언트가 그것을 조합하는 방식입니다. Next.js식 <code>'use server'</code> 액션은 <strong>의도적으로 지원하지 않고</strong> 명시적인 서버 함수만 씁니다.</li>
<li><strong>마이그레이션</strong>: RC → 1.0은 Vite 8 필수, <code>.inputValidator()</code> → <code>.validator()</code>, <code>useSession</code> 제거, Nitro 플러그인 교체, 에러·응답 동작 변경이 핵심입니다. Next.js App Router → Start는 공식 가이드와 before/after 예제가 있고, Remix 2 / React Router 7 가이드는 아직 "coming soon"입니다.</li>
<li><strong>보안 이력</strong>: 2026년 9월 30일 공개된 <strong>CVE-2026-102989</strong>(서버 함수 응답 처리의 반사형 XSS, CVSS 9.3). 실제로 설치된 <code>@tanstack/start-server-core</code>가 <strong>1.169.39 이상</strong>인지 확인하고 재배포해야 합니다. 5월 11일에는 42개 <code>@tanstack/*</code> 패키지에 악성 버전이 올라간 npm 공급망 공격(CVE-2026-45321)도 있었습니다.</li>
</ul>

































<table><thead><tr><th>항목</th><th>내용</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>버전</strong></td><td>1.0 = npm <code>1.169.x</code> (2026-10-13 공지)</td></tr><tr><td><strong>기반</strong></td><td>TanStack Router · Vite 8+ 또는 Rsbuild 2 · Node 22.12+</td></tr><tr><td><strong>핵심 API</strong></td><td><code>createServerFn</code> · <code>createMiddleware</code> · <code>createIsomorphicFn</code> · 서버 라우트</td></tr><tr><td><strong>렌더링</strong></td><td>SSR(기본) · data-only · client-only · SPA 모드 · 프리렌더링 · ISR</td></tr><tr><td><strong>배포</strong></td><td>Cloudflare Workers · Netlify · Railway · Vercel · Nitro · Node/Docker · Bun 등</td></tr><tr><td><strong>실험적 기능</strong></td><td>Server Components · Static Server Functions · Deferred Hydration</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>아래부터는 상세 내용입니다.</p>
<hr>

<h3 id="개요-tanstack-start는-무엇인가">개요: TanStack Start는 무엇인가<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#개요-tanstack-start는-무엇인가"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>TanStack Start는 <strong>TanStack Router로 움직이는 풀스택 React 프레임워크</strong> 입니다. 풀 도큐먼트 SSR, 스트리밍, 서버 함수, 클라이언트/서버 빌드를 제공하고, Vite와 Rsbuild를 지원하기 때문에 원하는 호스팅이나 런타임에 그대로 배포할 수 있습니다.</p>
<p>구성 요소는 두 개뿐입니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>TanStack Router</strong> — 중첩 라우팅, search params, 데이터 로딩을 타입 안전하게 다루는 라우터</li>
<li><strong>Vite 또는 Rsbuild</strong> — HMR과 프로덕션 빌드를 담당하는 빌드 도구</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="router만-쓸까-start를-쓸까">Router만 쓸까, Start를 쓸까<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#router만-쓸까-start를-쓸까"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>공식 문서는 "프레임워크의 90%는 라우터"라고 말합니다. Start는 라우팅을 <strong>전적으로</strong> TanStack Router에 맡기고, 그 위에 서버 쪽 기능을 얹습니다. 그래서 질문은 "서버 기능이 필요한가"로 바뀝니다.</p>



































<table><thead><tr><th>필요한 것</th><th align="center">Router만</th><th align="center">Start</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>타입 안전 라우팅 · search params · loader</td><td align="center">✅</td><td align="center">✅</td></tr><tr><td>풀 도큐먼트 SSR · 스트리밍</td><td align="center">△</td><td align="center">✅</td></tr><tr><td>서버 함수(타입 안전 RPC) · 서버 라우트(API)</td><td align="center">—</td><td align="center">✅</td></tr><tr><td>요청/서버 함수 미들웨어 · 컨텍스트</td><td align="center">—</td><td align="center">✅</td></tr><tr><td>클라이언트+서버 풀스택 빌드 · 유니버설 배포</td><td align="center">—</td><td align="center">✅</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>△는 TanStack Router에도 실험적인 SSR · 스트리밍 API가 있지만 직접 조립해야 한다는 뜻입니다. Start는 이를 기본 제공 형태로 묶어 줍니다. 위 기능이 <strong>하나도 필요 없다고 확신할 때만</strong> Router 단독(SPA)을 권한다는 게 공식 입장입니다.</p>
<h4 id="어떤-철학으로-만들었나">어떤 철학으로 만들었나<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#어떤-철학으로-만들었나"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>1.0 공지에서 Tanner Linsley가 밝힌 출발점은 분명합니다. React 생태계가 "애플리케이션은 이래야 한다"는 상을 한 가지로 좁히고 있다고 느꼈고 — Next.js는 RSC에 크게 베팅하며 서버 중심 모델로, React Router와 Remix는 각자의 변화로 — 자신은 <strong>지난 10년간 만들어 온 방식</strong>(상태 관리, 클라이언트 내비게이션, 데이터 캐싱)을 그대로 유지하면서 서버 렌더링 · 스트리밍 · 서버 함수 같은 새 기능을 <strong>필요한 곳에만</strong> 쓰고 싶었다는 것입니다.</p>
<p>그래서 Start의 기본값은 이렇습니다.</p>
<ul>
<li>컴포넌트는 그냥 컴포넌트, 라우트는 그냥 라우트입니다. <code>'use client'</code> 경계를 그릴 필요가 없습니다.</li>
<li>라우트는 서버에서 렌더링할 수도, 데이터만 서버에서 불러오고 컴포넌트는 클라이언트에서 그릴 수도, 전부 클라이언트에서 처리할 수도 있습니다.</li>
<li>서버가 필요하면 <strong>명시적인 API</strong>(<code>createServerFn</code>, 미들웨어)로 넘어갑니다.</li>
<li>RSC도 앱 모델을 바꾸지 않는 <strong>선택 기능</strong>으로 붙습니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="10까지-걸어온-길">1.0까지 걸어온 길<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#10까지-걸어온-길"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>













































<table><thead><tr><th>시점</th><th>사건</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>2023년 12월</td><td>저장소에 Start의 시초가 되는 서버 유틸리티 작업이 등장</td></tr><tr><td>2024년 5월</td><td><code>react-router-server</code> 패키지를 <code>start</code>로 이름 변경</td></tr><tr><td>2025년 6월</td><td><code>1.121.0</code> — Vinxi를 걷어내고 Vite 플러그인 구조로 전환("devinxi")</td></tr><tr><td>2025년 9월</td><td><code>1.132.0</code> 배포와 함께 "v1 Release Candidate" 공지(9/23)</td></tr><tr><td>2026년 4월</td><td>React Server Components를 실험 기능으로 공개(4/13, "React Server Components Your Way")</td></tr><tr><td>2026년 5월</td><td>npm 공급망 공격(CVE-2026-45321) — 악성 버전은 배포 당일 제거(5/11)</td></tr><tr><td>2026년 6월</td><td>Rsbuild 지원 공식화 · <code>.validator()</code>가 정식 이름으로 복귀</td></tr><tr><td>2026년 9월</td><td>CVE-2026-102989 보안 패치 공지(9/30)</td></tr><tr><td>2026년 10월</td><td><code>1.169.0</code> 배포, <strong>1.0 안정 버전 공지</strong>(10/13, KST)</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>1.0 공지에서 눈여겨볼 대목은 세 가지입니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>"이제야 RC라는 단서를 뗀다"</strong> — 작은 프로젝트부터 Fortune 500 기업까지(기업명은 밝히지 않았습니다) 베타·RC 단계에서 이미 프로덕션에 쓰고 있었고, 1.0은 그 API들에 대한 공식적인 약속이라는 설명입니다. RC 이후 "짧은 마무리"를 예상했지만 실제로는 컴파일러, 스트리밍, 서버 경계, 하이드레이션 작업이 꽤 많이 들어갔습니다.</li>
<li><strong>SSR 처리량 약 5.5배</strong> — 메인테이너들이 서버가 하지 않아도 되는 일을 프로파일링으로 걷어내, 자체 벤치마크에서 처리량을 약 5.5배로 끌어올렸다고 합니다. 다른 프레임워크와의 비교가 아니라 <strong>최적화 이전의 Start 대비</strong> 수치라는 점은 구분해 두는 게 좋습니다.</li>
<li><strong>에이전트와 타입</strong> — 라우터를 타입 안전하고 엄격하게 만든 설계가 에이전트 시대에도 그대로 이점이 된다는 주장입니다. 에이전트가 라우트 파라미터나 서버 함수 입력을 바꾸면 <strong>어긋난 곳을 타입이 찾아 준다</strong>는 것이죠. 같은 맥락에서 TanStack Intent로 npm 패키지 안에 에이전트용 스킬을 함께 배포하고 있습니다.</li>
</ul>
<p>RSC에 대한 솔직한 회고도 있습니다. 팀은 RSC를 Start의 앱 모델을 바꾸지 않는 방식으로 통합하는 데 많은 시간을 들였지만, 정작 tanstack.com에서는 "무엇이 어디로 배포되는지" 추적하기가 너무 복잡해져 <strong>RSC를 다시 걷어냈다</strong>고 합니다. 그리고 그걸 걷어내는 데 앱을 다시 짤 필요가 없었다는 점을 opt-in 설계의 근거로 듭니다.</p>
<h3 id="핵심-개념-이-코드는-어디서-실행되는가">핵심 개념: 이 코드는 어디서 실행되는가<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#핵심-개념-이-코드는-어디서-실행되는가"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>Start를 쓰면서 가장 먼저 익혀야 할 규칙은 하나입니다. <strong>모든 코드는 기본적으로 isomorphic</strong> 입니다. 따로 제한하지 않으면 서버 번들과 클라이언트 번들 <strong>양쪽에</strong> 들어갑니다.</p>
<p>특히 <strong>라우트 <code>loader</code>는 서버 전용이 아닙니다.</strong> 첫 요청(SSR)에서는 서버에서, 이후 클라이언트 내비게이션에서는 브라우저에서 실행됩니다. Next.js의 async Server Component나 React Router의 서버 <code>loader</code>에 익숙하다면 이 부분이 가장 큰 함정입니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ❌ 첫 요청(SSR)에선 동작하지만, 클라이언트 내비게이션 때는 브라우저에서 실행된다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//    브라우저 코드는 빌드 도구의 공개 접두사(VITE_ 등)가 붙은 변수만 읽을 수 있어서</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//    SECRET은 읽히지 않고, 이를 "고치려고" VITE_ 접두사를 붙이면 비밀값이 번들에 박힌다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createFileRoute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/users'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> secret</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> process.env.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">SECRET</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> res</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fetch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`https://api.example.com/users?key=${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">secret</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> res.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ✅ 비밀값이 필요한 로직은 서버 함수 안에서 읽고, loader는 그것을 호출만 한다</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> getUsers</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createServerFn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">handler</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> secret</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> process.env.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">SECRET</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> res</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fetch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`https://api.example.com/users?key=${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">secret</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> res.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createFileRoute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/users'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getUsers</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>공식 문서의 규칙은 간단합니다. 서버 코드는 접두사 없는 <code>process.env</code>를 읽을 수 있고, 클라이언트 코드는 빌드 도구의 공개 접두사(Vite <code>VITE_</code>, Rsbuild <code>PUBLIC_</code>)가 붙은 값만 읽습니다. 그러니 "loader에서 읽던 비밀값"은 접두사를 붙일 게 아니라 <strong>서버 함수 안으로 옮기는 것</strong>이 정답입니다.</p>
<p>실행 위치를 제어하는 API는 다음과 같습니다.</p>








































<table><thead><tr><th>API</th><th>용도</th><th>반대쪽 환경에서의 동작</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>createServerFn()</code></td><td>RPC, 데이터 변경</td><td>클라이언트에서는 서버로 네트워크 요청</td></tr><tr><td><code>createServerOnlyFn(fn)</code></td><td>서버 전용 유틸</td><td>클라이언트에서 호출하면 throw</td></tr><tr><td><code>createClientOnlyFn(fn)</code></td><td>브라우저 전용 유틸</td><td>서버에서 호출하면 throw</td></tr><tr><td><code>createIsomorphicFn().server().client()</code></td><td>환경별로 다른 구현</td><td>각 번들에서 반대쪽 구현이 제거됨</td></tr><tr><td><code>&#x3C;ClientOnly fallback></code></td><td>브라우저 API가 필요한 컴포넌트</td><td>서버에서는 fallback을 렌더</td></tr><tr><td><code>useHydrated()</code></td><td>하이드레이션 여부 분기</td><td>SSR과 첫 클라이언트 렌더에선 <code>false</code></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>1.0 공지에 실린 <code>createIsomorphicFn</code> 예시가 이 모델을 잘 보여줍니다. 현재 origin을 구하는 방법이 서버(요청 URL)와 브라우저(<code>window.location</code>)에서 다른데, 두 구현을 나란히 두면 Start가 각 번들에서 반대쪽 구현을 지워 줍니다. 브라우저에서 호출해도 서버로 요청이 가지 않습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createIsomorphicFn } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { getRequest } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start/server'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> getOrigin</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createIsomorphicFn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">server</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> URL</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getRequest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().url).origin)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">client</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> window.location.origin);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 서버 요청 중이든 브라우저든 같은 호출</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> absoluteUrl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> URL</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/about'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getOrigin</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()).href;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>파일 단위로 경계를 그을 수도 있습니다. <code>*.server.*</code> / <code>*.client.*</code> 접미사가 붙은 파일은 <strong>Import Protection</strong>(기본 활성화)이 반대쪽 번들에 들어가지 못하게 막고, 파일 이름을 바꿀 수 없다면 맨 위에 마커를 import 합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/lib/secrets.ts — 파일명을 *.server.ts로 바꿀 수 없을 때</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start/server-only'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getApiKey</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> process.env.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">API_KEY</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>한 가지 더, <strong>모듈 최상위에서 <code>process.env</code>를 읽지 마세요.</strong> 클라이언트 번들에 값이 인라인될 수 있을 뿐 아니라, Cloudflare Workers 같은 엣지 런타임은 env를 <strong>요청마다</strong> 주입하기 때문에 모듈 로드 시점에는 서버에서도 <code>undefined</code>가 됩니다. <code>.handler()</code>, 미들웨어 <code>.server()</code>, 서버 라우트 핸들러 안에서 읽어야 합니다.</p>
<h3 id="사용법">사용법<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#사용법"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<h4 id="1-프로젝트-만들기">1. 프로젝트 만들기<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#1-프로젝트-만들기"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>가장 빠른 길은 세 가지입니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>TanStack Builder</strong> — 공식 문서가 첫 번째로 권하는 AI 기반 셋업 흐름(<code>tanstack.com/builder</code>)</li>
<li><strong>CLI</strong> — 패키지 매니저와 Tailwind CSS · ESLint 같은 애드온을 고르며 생성</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @tanstack/cli@latest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> create</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li><strong>공식 예제에서 시작</strong> — <code>start-basic</code>, <code>start-basic-rsbuild</code>, <code>start-basic-auth</code>, <code>start-basic-react-query</code>, <code>start-clerk-basic</code>, <code>start-supabase-basic</code>, <code>start-workos</code> 등</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> gitpick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> TanStack/router/tree/main/examples/react/start-basic</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> start-basic</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">cd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> start-basic</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> install</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> run</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> dev</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4 id="2-직접-구성해-보기">2. 직접 구성해 보기<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#2-직접-구성해-보기"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>구조를 이해하려면 한 번은 손으로 만들어 보는 게 좋습니다. 필요한 파일은 많지 않습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @tanstack/react-start</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @tanstack/react-router</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> react-dom</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> -D</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> vite</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @vitejs/plugin-react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> typescript</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @types/react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @types/react-dom</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @types/node</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><code>package.json</code>은 ESM으로 두고 Vite CLI를 씁니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="json" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="json" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">  "type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"module"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">  "scripts"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">    "dev"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"vite dev"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">    "build"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"vite build"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><code>vite.config.ts</code>에서는 <strong>Start 플러그인이 React 플러그인보다 앞에</strong> 와야 합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// vite.config.ts</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { defineConfig } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'vite'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { tanstackStart } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start/plugin/vite'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> viteReact </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@vitejs/plugin-react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> defineConfig</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  server: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    port: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  resolve: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    tsconfigPaths: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  plugins: [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    tanstackStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // React 플러그인은 반드시 Start 플러그인 뒤에</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    viteReact</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ]</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">})</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Rsbuild를 쓴다면 <code>@rsbuild/core</code>와 <code>@rsbuild/plugin-react</code>를 설치하고 <code>@tanstack/react-start/plugin/rsbuild</code>의 <code>tanstackStart()</code>를 <code>pluginReact()</code>와 함께 등록하면 됩니다.</p>
<p><code>tsconfig.json</code>은 <code>jsx: "react-jsx"</code>, <code>moduleResolution: "Bundler"</code>, <code>module: "ESNext"</code>, <code>target: "ES2022"</code> 정도면 충분합니다. 단, <strong><code>verbatimModuleSyntax</code>는 켜지 마세요.</strong> 서버 번들이 클라이언트 번들로 새어 나갈 수 있다고 공식 문서가 경고합니다.</p>
<p>최소 구성의 파일 트리는 이렇습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="text" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="text" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span>src/</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>├── routes/</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>│   └── __root.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>├── router.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>└── routeTree.gen.ts   ← 처음 실행할 때 자동 생성</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>vite.config.ts</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>package.json</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>tsconfig.json</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><code>router.tsx</code>는 <strong>호출할 때마다 새 라우터를 돌려주는</strong> <code>getRouter</code>를 export 합니다. SSR 요청마다 라우터를 새로 만들기 때문입니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/router.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createRouter } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-router'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { routeTree } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> './routeTree.gen'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> router</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    routeTree,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    scrollRestoration: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> router;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>루트 라우트는 문서 셸(<code>&#x3C;html></code>, <code>&#x3C;head></code>, <code>&#x3C;body></code>)을 그리고, <code>&#x3C;head></code>에는 <code>HeadContent</code>를, <code>&#x3C;body></code> 끝에는 <code>Scripts</code>를 둡니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/routes/__root.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { ReactNode } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  Outlet,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  createRootRoute,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  HeadContent,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  Scripts,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-router'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createRootRoute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  head</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    meta: [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      { charSet: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'utf-8'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'viewport'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, content: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'width=device-width, initial-scale=1'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      { title: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'TanStack Start Starter'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  component: RootComponent,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> RootComponent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">RootDocument</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Outlet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">RootDocument</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> RootDocument</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Readonly</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;{ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> ReactNode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }>) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">html</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">head</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">HeadContent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">head</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">body</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Scripts</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">body</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">html</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4 id="3-첫-라우트-loader와-서버-함수">3. 첫 라우트: loader와 서버 함수<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#3-첫-라우트-loader와-서버-함수"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>공식 튜토리얼의 카운터 예제는 Start의 기본 흐름을 한 파일에 담고 있습니다. <strong>GET 서버 함수로 읽고, POST 서버 함수로 쓰고, loader로 초기 데이터를 넣고, 변경 후 <code>router.invalidate()</code>로 다시 불러옵니다.</strong></p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/routes/index.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fs </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'node:fs'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createFileRoute, useRouter } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-router'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createServerFn } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> filePath</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'count.txt'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> readCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> parseInt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fs.promises.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">readFile</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(filePath, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'utf-8'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '0'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> getCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createServerFn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ method: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'GET'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">handler</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> readCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> updateCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createServerFn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ method: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'POST'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">validator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> d)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">handler</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> count</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> readCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fs.promises.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">writeFile</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(filePath, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">count</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createFileRoute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  component: Home,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Home</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> router</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> state</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> Route.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">useLoaderData</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"button"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      onClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">        updateCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ data: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          router.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">invalidate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      Add 1 to </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">state</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">?</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><code>node:fs</code>를 import 했는데도 클라이언트 번들이 깨지지 않는 이유는, 빌드가 클라이언트 쪽에서 서버 함수 구현을 <strong>RPC 스텁으로 바꿔치기</strong> 하기 때문입니다. 브라우저에서 <code>updateCount()</code>를 부르면 <code>fetch</code> 요청이 되고, 실제 코드는 서버에만 남습니다.</p>
<h4 id="4-서버-함수-제대로-쓰기">4. 서버 함수 제대로 쓰기<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#4-서버-함수-제대로-쓰기"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>서버 함수는 <code>data</code> 인자 하나를 받습니다. 네트워크 경계를 넘으므로 <strong>런타임 검증</strong>이 기본이고, Zod 같은 스키마를 그대로 넘길 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createServerFn } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { z } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'zod'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> UserSchema</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> z.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">object</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  name: z.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">min</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  age: z.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">min</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> createUser</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createServerFn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ method: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'POST'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">validator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(UserSchema)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">handler</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // data는 검증과 타입 추론이 끝난 상태</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> `Created user: ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}, age ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><code>redirect()</code>를 throw 하면 loader · beforeLoad 같은 라우트 생명주기와 <code>useServerFn()</code> 양쪽에서 자동으로 이동 처리됩니다. <code>notFound()</code>는 loader · beforeLoad에서 던질 때 <code>notFoundComponent</code>로 이어집니다. 공식 문서는 <code>useServerFn()</code>도 둘 다 처리한다고 설명하지만, 현재 <code>useServerFn</code> 소스는 redirect만 처리하므로 <strong>이벤트 핸들러에서 부른 서버 함수의 <code>notFound()</code>는 일반 에러로 받게</strong> 됩니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createServerFn } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { notFound } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-router'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> getPost</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createServerFn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">validator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> data)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">handler</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> post</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> db.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">findPost</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(data.id);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">post) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      throw</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> notFound</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> post;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>규모가 커지면 공식 문서가 제안하는 파일 규칙을 따르는 게 편합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="text" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="text" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span>src/utils/</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>├── users.functions.ts   # createServerFn 래퍼 — 어디서 import 해도 안전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>├── users.server.ts      # DB 쿼리 등 서버 전용 헬퍼 — 서버 함수 핸들러 안에서만 사용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>└── schemas.ts           # 클라이언트에서도 쓰는 검증 스키마</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>서버 함수는 정적 import 하면 어디서든 안전하지만 <strong>동적 <code>import()</code>는 번들러 문제를 일으킬 수 있으니 피하라</strong>고 합니다.</p>
<p>알아 둘 기본값도 있습니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>CSRF 보호</strong> — 서버 함수는 같은 출처(same-origin) RPC입니다. <code>src/start.ts</code>가 없으면 Start가 <code>createCsrfMiddleware()</code>를 자동으로 설치하지만, <strong><code>src/start.ts</code>를 직접 만들면 이 미들웨어를 명시적으로 추가해야 합니다.</strong> 이 미들웨어는 <code>Sec-Fetch-Site</code> · <code>Origin</code> · <code>Referer</code> 헤더로 출처를 확인하고 세 헤더가 모두 없는 요청은 기본으로 거부하므로, 이런 헤더를 지우는 프록시 뒤에 배포한다면 미리 확인해 두세요.</li>
<li><strong>직렬화 타입 검사</strong> — 입력과 반환값이 직렬화 가능한지 TypeScript가 검사합니다(<code>strict</code> 기본). <code>FormData</code>(POST)와 <code>Response</code>는 허용됩니다.</li>
<li><strong>캐시 헤더</strong> — <code>setResponseHeaders</code>로 <code>Cache-Control</code>을 지정할 수 있는데, 세션이나 쿠키에 따라 달라지는 응답에 <code>public</code>을 쓰면 <strong>다른 사용자에게 응답이 재생될 수 있으니</strong> <code>private</code>(+ <code>Vary</code>) 또는 <code>no-store</code>를 써야 합니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/start.ts — 이 파일을 만들었다면 CSRF 미들웨어를 직접 등록</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createStart, createCsrfMiddleware } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> csrfMiddleware</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createCsrfMiddleware</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">ctx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ctx.handlerType </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'serverFn'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> startInstance</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  requestMiddleware: [csrfMiddleware],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4 id="5-미들웨어">5. 미들웨어<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#5-미들웨어"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>미들웨어는 두 종류입니다.</p>






























<table><thead><tr><th>구분</th><th>요청 미들웨어</th><th>서버 함수 미들웨어</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>범위</td><td>모든 서버 요청(SSR, 서버 라우트, 서버 함수)</td><td>서버 함수만</td></tr><tr><td>메서드</td><td><code>.server()</code></td><td><code>.client()</code>, <code>.server()</code></td></tr><tr><td>입력 검증</td><td>없음</td><td><code>.validator()</code></td></tr><tr><td>클라이언트 로직</td><td>없음</td><td>있음(RPC 전후)</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>가장 흔한 쓰임은 인증입니다. 세션 조회를 미들웨어로 모아 두고, 비공개 데이터를 다루는 <strong>모든</strong> 서버 함수에 붙입니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/server/auth-middleware.ts</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createMiddleware } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { readSessionToken } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> './session'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> authMiddleware</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createMiddleware</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ type: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'function'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">server</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> token</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> readSessionToken</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> session</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> token </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> db.sessions.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">findValid</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(token) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">session) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">throw</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Unauthorized'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ context: { session } });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createServerFn } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
