<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { authMiddleware } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '~/server/auth-middleware'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> getMyOrders</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createServerFn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ method: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'GET'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">middleware</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([authMiddleware])</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">handler</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">context</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // context.session이 타입과 함께 들어온다</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> db.orders.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">findMany</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ where: { userId: context.session.userId } });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>공식 문서가 여러 번 강조하는 문장이 있습니다. <strong>라우트 가드(<code>beforeLoad</code>)는 데이터 인가 경계가 아닙니다.</strong> 서버 함수와 서버 라우트는 그것을 부르는 화면과 무관하게 <strong>독립적으로 호출 가능한 API 엔드포인트</strong>이므로, 인가는 핸들러나 미들웨어에서 해야 합니다. <code>beforeLoad</code>의 리다이렉트는 UX일 뿐입니다.</p>
<p>모든 요청이나 모든 서버 함수에 적용할 전역 미들웨어는 <code>src/start.ts</code>의 <code>createStart</code>에 <code>requestMiddleware</code> / <code>functionMiddleware</code> 배열로 등록합니다. 클라이언트 → 서버로 컨텍스트를 보내는 <code>sendContext</code>는 타입은 맞춰 주지만 <strong>런타임 검증은 하지 않으므로</strong>, 서버 미들웨어에서 다시 검증해야 합니다. 세션처럼 신뢰가 필요한 값은 절대 <code>sendContext</code>에서 꺼내면 안 됩니다.</p>
<h4 id="6-서버-라우트api-엔드포인트">6. 서버 라우트(API 엔드포인트)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#6-서버-라우트api-엔드포인트"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>외부에서 호출할 HTTP 엔드포인트(웹훅, 공개 API, 파일 응답 등)는 <strong>서버 라우트</strong>로 만듭니다. 앱 라우트와 같은 <code>src/routes</code> 디렉터리에, 같은 파일 규칙으로 둡니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/routes/hello.ts</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createFileRoute } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-router'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createFileRoute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/hello'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  server: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    handlers: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">      GET</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">request</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hello, World! from '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> request.url);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>





















<table><thead><tr><th>언제</th><th>무엇을</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Start 앱 안에서 부르고, 직렬화를 맡기고 싶다</td><td>서버 함수</td></tr><tr><td>앱 바깥(다른 서비스, 웹훅, 크롤러)에서 부른다</td><td>서버 라우트</td></tr><tr><td>둘 다 필요하다</td><td>같은 파일에 <code>component</code>와 <code>server</code>를 함께</td></tr></tbody></table>
<h4 id="7-tanstack-query와-함께-쓰기">7. TanStack Query와 함께 쓰기<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#7-tanstack-query와-함께-쓰기"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>Router loader가 내비게이션을 조율하고, Query가 데이터의 신선도와 뮤테이션 상태를 맡는 조합입니다. <code>@tanstack/react-query</code>와 <code>@tanstack/react-router-ssr-query</code>를 설치하고, <strong><code>QueryClient</code>를 <code>getRouter</code> 안에서 만듭니다.</strong> 모듈 최상위에 두면 SSR 요청 사이에 캐시가 공유되어 한 사용자의 데이터가 다른 사용자의 HTML에 섞일 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/router.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { QueryClient } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-query'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createRouter } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-router'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { setupRouterSsrQueryIntegration } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-router-ssr-query'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { routeTree } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> './routeTree.gen'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> queryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> QueryClient</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    defaultOptions: { queries: { staleTime: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">30_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> router</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    routeTree,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    context: { queryClient },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    defaultPreload: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'intent'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    defaultPreloadStaleTime: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setupRouterSsrQueryIntegration</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ router, queryClient });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> router;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>자식 라우트의 loader에서 <code>context.queryClient</code>를 쓰려면 루트 라우트를 <code>createRootRouteWithContext&#x3C;{ queryClient: QueryClient }>()</code>로 만들어 컨텍스트 타입을 선언합니다. Query Provider와 SSR dehydrate/hydrate는 통합 패키지가 맡으므로 직접 추가하지 않습니다.</p>
<p>loader와 컴포넌트가 <strong>같은 <code>queryOptions</code></strong> 를 공유하면, loader가 채운 캐시를 컴포넌트가 읽고 통합 패키지가 브라우저로 넘겨 줍니다. 하이드레이션 뒤에 같은 요청이 다시 나가지 않습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/routes/preferences.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { useSuspenseQuery } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-query'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createFileRoute } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-router'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { readerNameOptions } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '../utils/preferences'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createFileRoute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/preferences'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">context</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> context.queryClient.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">query</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(readerNameOptions);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  component: Preferences,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Preferences</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useSuspenseQuery</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(readerNameOptions);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Hello, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>뮤테이션 후에는 <strong>데이터의 실제 주인</strong>을 무효화해야 합니다. <code>queryClient.invalidateQueries</code>는 Query 캐시를, <code>router.invalidate()</code>는 loader 데이터와 라우트 컨텍스트를 다시 불러옵니다. 어느 쪽도 CDN이나 DB 캐시를 비우지는 않습니다. 이전에 정리한 <a href="/ko/posts/2023-07-23-react-query-v5/">react-query(v5)</a>의 <code>queryOptions</code> 패턴이 여기서 그대로 쓰입니다.</p>
<h3 id="렌더링-모드-고르기">렌더링 모드 고르기<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#렌더링-모드-고르기"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>Start의 가장 실용적인 장점은 렌더링 방식을 <strong>라우트 단위로</strong> 고를 수 있다는 점입니다.</p>















































<table><thead><tr><th>모드</th><th>설정</th><th>첫 요청 때 서버가 하는 일</th><th>어울리는 화면</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Full SSR</strong>(기본)</td><td><code>ssr: true</code></td><td><code>beforeLoad</code> · <code>loader</code> 실행 + 컴포넌트 렌더</td><td>SEO와 첫 화면이 중요한 페이지</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Data-only</strong></td><td><code>ssr: 'data-only'</code></td><td><code>beforeLoad</code> · <code>loader</code>만 실행</td><td>데이터는 서버에서, 컴포넌트는 브라우저 API 의존</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Client-only</strong></td><td><code>ssr: false</code></td><td>실행하지 않음(fallback만 렌더)</td><td>loader가 <code>localStorage</code> 등에 의존</td></tr><tr><td><strong>SPA 모드</strong></td><td><code>tanstackStart({ spa: { enabled: true } })</code></td><td>빌드 때 루트 셸을 프리렌더(<code>/_shell.html</code>)</td><td>CDN 정적 호스팅, SEO가 필요 없는 앱</td></tr><tr><td><strong>정적 프리렌더링</strong></td><td><code>tanstackStart({ prerender: { enabled: true } })</code></td><td>빌드 때 HTML 생성</td><td>블로그, 문서, 마케팅 페이지</td></tr><tr><td><strong>ISR</strong></td><td><code>Cache-Control</code> + CDN</td><td>캐시 만료 후 다음 요청에서 재생성</td><td>자주 바뀌지 않는 공개 페이지</td></tr></tbody></table>
<h4 id="selective-ssr">Selective SSR<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#selective-ssr"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/routes/posts/$postId.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createFileRoute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/posts/$postId'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ssr: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'data-only'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // 첫 요청에서는 서버, 이후 내비게이션에서는 클라이언트</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  component</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>이 컴포넌트는 클라이언트에서 렌더링된다&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><code>ssr</code>은 함수로도 줄 수 있습니다. 검증이 끝난 <code>params</code>와 <code>search</code>를 받아 요청마다 결정하고, 이 함수는 서버에서만 실행되며 클라이언트 번들에서는 제거됩니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createFileRoute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/docs/$docType/$docId'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  validateSearch: z.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">object</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ details: z.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">boolean</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">optional</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() }),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  ssr</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">search</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (params.status </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'success'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> params.value.docType </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'sheet'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (search.status </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'success'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> search.value.details) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'data-only'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>상속 규칙</strong>이 중요합니다. 자식 라우트는 부모 설정을 물려받고, <strong>더 엄격한 쪽으로만</strong> 바꿀 수 있습니다(<code>true</code> → <code>'data-only'</code> → <code>false</code>). 부모가 <code>ssr: false</code>면 자식에 <code>ssr: true</code>를 써도 효과가 없습니다. 기본값 자체를 바꾸려면 <code>src/start.ts</code>의 <code>createStart(() => ({ defaultSsr: false }))</code>를 씁니다. 그리고 이 설정은 <strong>첫 요청</strong>에만 해당합니다. 이후 클라이언트 내비게이션에서는 모드와 상관없이 <code>beforeLoad</code>와 <code>loader</code>가 브라우저에서 실행됩니다.</p>
<h4 id="spa-모드">SPA 모드<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#spa-모드"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>SSR이 없다고 서버 기능을 포기하는 건 아닙니다. 빌드 후 루트 라우트를 프리렌더해 <code>/_shell.html</code>을 만들고(매칭된 라우트 자리에는 pending 컴포넌트가 들어갑니다), 서버 함수와 서버 라우트는 그대로 쓸 수 있습니다. 다른 라우트도 함께 프리렌더할 수 있고, 공식 문서는 가능한 한 많이 프리렌더하라고 권합니다. 대신 호스팅 쪽에 리라이트 규칙이 필요합니다(Netlify <code>_redirects</code> 예).</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="text" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="text" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span># 서버 함수와 API는 서버로 통과</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>/_serverFn/* /_serverFn/:splat 200</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>/api/* /api/:splat 200</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span># 나머지 404는 SPA 셸로</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>/* /_shell.html 200</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4 id="정적-프리렌더링">정적 프리렌더링<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#정적-프리렌더링"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// vite.config.ts</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">tanstackStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  prerender: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    enabled: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    crawlLinks: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// HTML에서 링크를 따라가며 함께 프리렌더</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    concurrency: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">14</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    failOnError: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> !</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">path.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">startsWith</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/do-not-render-me'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4 id="server-components실험적">Server Components(실험적)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#server-components실험적"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>RSC는 기본으로 꺼져 있고, 켜려면 React 19+, Vite 8+ 또는 Rsbuild 2가 필요합니다. Vite에서는 <code>@vitejs/plugin-rsc</code>를 설치하고 <code>tanstackStart({ rsc: { enabled: true } })</code>와 <code>rsc()</code> 플러그인을 함께 등록합니다. 사용 방식은 "서버 함수가 서버 컴포넌트를 렌더링해 돌려주고, loader가 그것을 받는" 형태입니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createFileRoute } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-router'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { createServerFn } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { renderServerComponent } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start/rsc'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Greeting</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Hello from RSC&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> getGreeting</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createServerFn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">handler</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Renderable</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> renderServerComponent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Greeting</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { Renderable };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createFileRoute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Renderable</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getGreeting</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { Greeting: Renderable };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  component: HomePage,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> HomePage</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Greeting</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> Route.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">useLoaderData</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Greeting</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>슬롯이 필요하면 <code>createCompositeComponent</code>로 만든 소스를 <code>&#x3C;CompositeComponent src={...}></code>로 렌더링하고, 그 안에 클라이언트 컴포넌트를 children으로 끼워 넣습니다. 서버가 만든 UI를 클라이언트가 조합하는 구조라서, 컴포넌트 트리의 주인이 여전히 클라이언트 쪽 라우터라는 점이 Next.js와 다릅니다.</p>
<p>알아 둘 제약도 있습니다. RSC는 React의 Flight 프로토콜을 그대로 쓰기 때문에 서버 컴포넌트 안에서는 Start 고유의 직렬화를 아직 쓸 수 없고(원시값 · Date · React 요소는 가능), TanStack Query로 서버 컴포넌트를 캐시할 때는 <code>structuralSharing: false</code>가 필수입니다.</p>
<p>RSC를 공개한 글("React Server Components Your Way", 2026-04-13)은 <strong><code>'use server'</code> 액션을 의도적으로 지원하지 않는다</strong>고 밝힙니다. 이미 알려진 공격 벡터가 있고, 네트워크 경계를 암묵적으로 만든다는 이유입니다. 같은 글은 Start가 클라이언트에서 온 Flight 데이터를 파싱하지 않기 때문에 다른 RSC 프레임워크를 덮친 최근 CVE가 해당하지 않는다고 설명하는데, 독립 감사가 아니라 TanStack의 자체 평가라는 점은 감안해야 합니다.</p>
<p>공식 비교 문서는 이 기능을 <strong>"Next.js와 동등한 프로덕션 기능처럼 소개하면 안 된다"</strong> 고 직접 선을 긋고 있습니다. RSC가 앱의 중심이라면 이 점을 먼저 고려해야 합니다.</p>
<h3 id="배포">배포<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#배포"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>Start는 <strong>어떤 호스팅에서도 동작하도록</strong> 설계됐고, 공식 호스팅 파트너는 Cloudflare · Netlify · Railway입니다. 문서에 가이드가 있는 대상은 Cloudflare Workers, Netlify, Railway, Nitro, Vercel, Node.js/Docker, Bun, Appwrite Sites입니다.</p>
<p>가장 범용적인 방법은 <strong>Nitro</strong> 입니다. Vite Environment API와 네이티브로 통합되며, Nitro가 지원하는 수많은 호스팅으로 배포할 수 있습니다(문서에는 아직 활발히 개발 중이라는 경고가 붙어 있습니다).</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// vite.config.ts</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { tanstackStart } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@tanstack/react-start/plugin/vite'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { defineConfig } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'vite'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { nitro } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'nitro/vite'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> viteReact </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@vitejs/plugin-react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> defineConfig</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  plugins: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">tanstackStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">nitro</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">viteReact</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()]</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">})</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Cloudflare Workers는 <code>@cloudflare/vite-plugin</code>과 <code>wrangler</code>를 쓰고, SSR 환경을 Workers로 지정합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="ts" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// vite.config.ts</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ]</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">})</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="json" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="json" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span></span>
